# Graph Report - pikar-ai  (2026-08-26)

## Corpus Check
- 2077 files · ~4,061,827 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 19641 nodes · 23275 edges · 1873 communities (1721 shown, 152 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 100% EXTRACTED · 0% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 92 edges (avg confidence: 0.72)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `845f4b12`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
